# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-17 through 2026-10-23

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-17 — Brooklyn Pop-Up x Old Stone House Fall Artisans Bazaar and Traditional Makers Fair + Runner Up

**Brooklyn Pop-Up x Old Stone House Fall Artisans Bazaar and Traditional Makers Fair, then a meal.** Wine bar and small plates at Runner Up. It is a 9-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=336+3rd+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=367+7th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/brooklyn-pop-up-osh-fall-artisans-bazaar-traditional-makers-fair-tickets-2002632772532) · [Restaurant](https://www.runnerupbk.com/)

### 2026-10-21 — Somssi + Robert Glasper Experiment with Questlove and Bilal

**Dinner, then Robert Glasper Experiment with Questlove and Bilal.** Korean-influenced bistro at Somssi. It is a 3-minute walk from the venue. Robert Glasper Experiment with Questlove and Bilal starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=79+MacDougal+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&destination=131+W+3rd+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://www.bluenote.net) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43377431-robert-glasper-experiment-at-blue-note-jazz-club)

### 2026-10-17 — 36th Tompkins Square Halloween Dog Parade + Good Time Country Buffet

**36th Tompkins Square Halloween Dog Parade, then a meal.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=EAST+13+STREET+between+AVENUE+A+and+AVENUE+B%2C+AVENUE+B+between+EAST+13+STREET+and+EAST+11+STREET%2C+AVENUE+B+between+EAST+11+STREET+and+EAST+7+STREET%2C+EAST+7+STREET+between+AVENUE+B+and+AVENUE+C%2C+EAST+7+STREET+between+AVENUE+B+and+AVENUE+A&destination=166+First+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nydogparade.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/) · [Reservations](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### 2026-10-22 — Noah Gundersen with Runnner and Breakup Shoes + Somssi

**Noah Gundersen with Runnner and Breakup Shoes, then dinner.** Korean-influenced bistro at Somssi. It is a 2-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-10-18 — Leland Eating and Drinking House + I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series

**Dinner, then I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series.** New American at Leland Eating and Drinking House. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

### 2026-10-19 — C as in Charlie + Amber Mark

**Dinner, then Amber Mark.** Korean-Southern small plates at C as in Charlie. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. Amber Mark starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=5+Bleecker+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&destination=131+West+Third+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://www.bluenote.net) · [Event booking](https://www.ticketweb.com/event/amber-mark-blue-note-jazz-club-tickets/14292404?REFERRAL_ID=tmfeedbuyat234705&wt.mc_id=aff_BUYAT_234705&camefrom=CFC_BUYAT_234705) · [Restaurant](https://www.c-asincharlie.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Brooklyn Synth & Pedal Expo + Foul Witch

**Brooklyn Synth & Pedal Expo, then a meal.** Italian wine bar at Foul Witch. It is 2.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=100+Sutton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&destination=15+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://synthandpedalexpo.com/brooklyn-synth-pedal-expo/) · [Restaurant](https://www.foulwitchnyc.com/)

## What to do this week

### 2026-10-17

#### [Brooklyn Pop-Up x Old Stone House Fall Artisans Bazaar and Traditional Makers Fair](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/brooklyn-pop-up-osh-fall-artisans-bazaar-traditional-makers-fair-tickets-2002632772532)

10:00 AM · markets · Free · Old Stone House and Washington Park · Park Slope · Brooklyn · Score 85.0  
Two linked outdoor markets bring 100-plus local artisans to Old Stone House, with traditional craft techniques including print design, glassblowing, ceramics, woodworking, metalwork, weaving, knitting, and crochet.
**Timing:** 10am-5pm; extreme-weather date Sunday, October 18.
**Practical note:** Outdoor market; NWS forecast is cool and breezy with only early shower chance.
**Why now:** Annual fall bazaar and new traditional makers fair take place October 17.

5:00 PM · museums · Free · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Score 54.0  
The Whitney is free to all every Friday evening from 5 to 10pm, and also on the second Sunday of each month.
**Timing:** Free admission Fridays 5-10pm. Free tickets are limited and released in advance.
**Practical note:** Reserve the free timed ticket ahead; walk-up availability runs out on busy evenings.
**Why now:** A standing free window that makes a good anchor for a Friday evening in the Meatpacking District.
[Official](https://whitney.org/visit)

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public source evidence and a small number of direct public-page checks; kept actionable restaurant, menu, deal and dated-event items, while rejecting duplicate or not-yet-open watch-list cards.

Dining research: 2026-10-17T10:36:54+00:00

No new Michelin awards are retained. Eater describes Jeju Noodle Bar’s West Village restaurant as Michelin-starred, but the Nolita item is treated only as a second-location opening, not as an awarded restaurant; no other unsourced Michelin claims were added.

### Oyster Hour: $10 half-dozen oysters at The Fulton

**Practical note:** Use the official ticket link from Broadway.org/Broadway Direct for date-specific availability.
**Why now:** The limited holiday Broadway engagement has an official closing date inside the book-ahead window.
**Book soon:** Broadway.org lists a finite Broadway holiday run closing January 3, 2027, so holiday-week performances will disappear after that date.
[Official](https://www.blueman.com/holiday-tour) · [Tickets / booking](https://tickets.broadwaydirect.com/shop/tickets/series/258362) · [Source: Broadway.org](https://www.broadway.org/shows/blue-man-group-a-new-holiday-surprise)
